- **chronon-api-rate-limit-hit-mid-backfil-e26b09bc1b6b5f02**: Poll the backfill status with growing intervals and stop hammering the status endpoint, resuming from the last known job state, because the monitor itself exhausted the API rate limit by polling a long backfill too aggressively and then could not read the status it needed. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon API rate limit hit mid-backfill monitor error agent failed to poll status. Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-api-rate-limit-hit-mid-backfil-e26b09bc1b6b5f02))
- **chronon-gi**: Unified light transport using the chronon coupling function κ(r). Use when implementing global illumination, rendering, path tracing, or any light transport system. The coupling function IS the Green's function derived from the Matryoshka-Chronon temporal shell framework—it encodes ALL transport in ONE evaluation. Replaces separate calculations for direct light, indirect bounces, shadows, caustics, SSS, and volumetrics. Triggers on requests for GI implementations, real-time rendering, path tracers, or physically-based light transport. ([jenkinsm13/physics-research-dump](https://github.com/jenkinsm13/physics-research-dump/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-gi))
- **chronon-group-by-backfill-failed-error-8921ae9b7a2a3caf**: Confirm the source table actually holds rows for that partition date, then rerun the backfill for just that partition, because the staging query produced no output either because the upstream partition landed late or because the partition filter in the conf points at a date with no source rows. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon group by backfill failed error "staging query produced no output for partition". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-group-by-backfill-failed-error-8921ae9b7a2a3caf))
- **chronon-join-failed-error-left-source-34acd0389d4bdf10**: Deduplicate the left source on the join keys plus the partition column before the join runs, keeping the latest row per key by event timestamp, because Chronon joins require the left side to be unique on its keys and duplicate keys make the point-in-time result ambiguous. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon join failed error "left source has duplicate keys for join". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-join-failed-error-left-source-34acd0389d4bdf10))
- **chronon-upload-failed-error-conf-file-987751bb78163951**: Add at least one group_by to the join conf and run the conf validation again before uploading, because a join with no group_bys has no features to compute and the validator rejects it at upload rather than letting a featureless join deploy. Use when you see this exact failure: Chronon upload failed error "conf file validation failed: join has no group_bys". Not for a vendor wide outage or a different error message. ([Vectle/skills](https://github.com/Vectle/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chronon-upload-failed-error-conf-file-987751bb78163951))

Trigger when the user wants to plan their day or week, schedule tasks, create a daily timetable, or asks how to organize today's work — especially phrases like "help me plan my day," "schedule my tasks," "what should I do today," "plan my week," or "make me a daily plan." - **chronoroot2-segmentation-eval**: Evaluates deep learning models for simultaneous multi-organ segmentation in 2D plant images. It probes the model's ability to accurately delineate root systems and aerial parts across different plant species and light conditions, while preserving structural fidelity for downstream phenotypic analysis. Use when the user wants to benchmark on Arabidopsis thaliana 2D phenotyping dataset, Tomato 2D phenotyping dataset, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports Dice coefficient. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/chronoroot2-segmentation-eval) · ★3)
- **chronos**: Set up, check, pause, or remove Chronos on Windows and explain its privacy controls. - **chuan-bi-cuoc-hop**: Lập Hồ sơ chuẩn bị họp trước buổi họp: mục đích và loại họp, câu hỏi cần quyết, tài liệu đọc trước, cam kết cũ còn mở, người dự theo vai trò và đủ người quyết, chương trình có thời lượng, cách điều phối, mức sẵn sàng; đề xuất hủy họp hoặc trao đổi bằng văn bản khi không đáng họp. Dùng khi nghe "chuẩn bị họp", "lên chương trình họp", "tài liệu đọc trước", "agenda họp", "họp này cần chuẩn bị gì". Không thuộc Skill này: gửi lời mời, dời lịch; ghi biên bản sau họp; quyết định thay người chủ trì. Ba chế độ: chuẩn bị nhanh, chuẩn bị đầy đủ, tập dượt tình huống. Dùng khi nghe "chuẩn bị đàm phán", "đàm phán lại giá với nhà cung cấp", "chiến lược nhượng bộ", "BATNA". ([tranvanhuy-hichan/ai-nestor-agent](https://github.com/tranvanhuy-hichan/ai-nestor-agent/tree/HEAD/app/backend/nestor/bundled_skills/chuan-hoa-sau-sap-nhap) · ★1)
- **chuan-xi-lu**: 知行合一时调用此skill。当用户需要将认知转化为行动、在日常事务中修行、克服"知道但做不到"的困境、或想深入实践王阳明心学时使用。触发信号："知道做不到怎么办"、"怎么在事上磨练"、"良知怎么致"、"怎么格物"、"心学怎么实践"、"怎么去除私欲"。不适用于：纯理论学习、不涉及行动的场景、需要外部方法论的场景。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/chuan-xi-lu) · ★1)
- **chuan-xi-zong-gang**: 何时调用：当用户需要一套完整的"从认知觉醒到日常修行"的体系，或多个传习录 skill 同时适用时。 关键信号：用户同时需要"心即理+知行合一+致良知+事上磨练"等多个方面的帮助。 何时不调用：用户只需要单一方面的帮助（用对应的单一 skill 即可）。 ([jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills](https://github.com/jianjian12138/cangjie-classics-skills/tree/HEAD/chuan-xi-lu/chuan-xi-zong-gang) · ★1)
- **chuanban-antigravity-theme-patch**: 船板的 Antigravity 2.0 主题、字体与汉化补丁（v5.5.3 · 适配 Antigravity 2.34.0+ / 2.33.0+ macOS / Windows / Linux 单图标 M1+M2 零子进程空闲短路启动接力 + Windows 5 分钟保活与更新后快捷方式自愈 + Browser 级 CDP 事件驱动毫秒级发现 + UI 西文 Google Sans Flex 可变字体引擎 × 中文更纱黑体跨平台零卡顿映射 + 对话正文 Anthropic Serif 衬线 + 昼夜双模式四套预设与外观面板 📖 书卷切换钮 + 宿主独占指纹门禁 + WSL2 双轨只读 SQLite + 1,940 条零冲突词典 + 分屏/支线提问(/btw)/子Agent侧栏独立实时指标胶囊 + 系统设置「本地扩展 → Token 统计」全量分析面板）。采用「core/ 只读内核 + user/ 永不覆盖改装层 + install.mjs CLI」，支持 Agent 一键部署、2.5 秒热重载定制与 --safe-mode 沙箱排障。当用户提到“安装船板 Antigravity 主题”、“升级/修复 Antigravity 汉化与字体”、“定制 Antigravity 界面样式或汉化词条”时触发。 ([Anarcadia/chuanban-antigravity-theme-patch](https://github.com/Anarcadia/chuanban-antigravity-theme-patch/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★522)
- **chuanban-antigravity-theme-patch**: 船板的 Antigravity 2.0 主题、字体与深度汉化全栈成品补丁包（v5.1.4 模块化架构 · 完整适配 Antigravity 2.31.0 双端全量演进 + 昼夜双模式独立解耦 + SQLite O(1) 增量遥测与 WeakMap 热路径隔离缓存）。采用「core/ 只读黄金内核 + user/ 永不覆盖改装层 + install.mjs 一键生命周期 CLI」，支持对方 Agent 零代码生成一键部署、2.5 秒热重载二次定制与 --safe-mode 沙箱排障。当用户提到“安装船板 Antigravity 主题”、“升级/修复 Antigravity 汉化与字体”、“定制 Antigravity 界面样式或汉化词条”时触发。 ([whyhhh20/antigravity-subagent-progress-dock](https://github.com/whyhhh20/antigravity-subagent-progress-dock/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **chuanfan-seo-gate**: 基于可验证证据审计、诊断、修复并验收网站 SEO 与 AI 搜索可见性基础。用户提到网站开发后的 SEO 优化、收录或索引异常、自然流量下降、robots.txt、sitemap、canonical、redirect、metadata、JSON-LD、hreflang、内链、JavaScript 渲染、Core Web Vitals、Search Console、网站迁移、Google AI Overviews、ChatGPT Search、Copilot 或 Perplexity 可见性时使用。审计、检查、诊断、比较、建议以及含糊的“优化一下”默认只读；只有明确要求修复或实施时才能修改指定范围。提交、推送、部署、站长平台写入、删页和索引控制变更仍需分别授权。不用于付费广告、ASO、垃圾外链、关键词堆砌、排名保证或缺少证据的流量归因。 ([chuanfan-ai/chuanfan-seo-gate](https://github.com/chuanfan-ai/chuanfan-seo-gate/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))

- **chuanzhang-changjing**: 给 AI 短剧出美术设定集（场景 + 叙事道具）：场景的设计意图、一致性锚点、光照时段变体、 空景提示词；道具的戏剧功能、状态变体、尺度参照、白底无手提示词。 产出 art.json + Markdown + 单页评审报告（含导出 JSON）。 为 AI 生成而设计，不是实拍——环境和道具都是生成资产，交付的是让它们跨集长一样的一致性方案； 10 道质量门全部由脚本确定性检查（锚点 3–5、无人无手、白底可抠、尺度短语、提示词英文……）。 有 chuanzhang-dagang 的 outline.json 就用 seed 预填场景清单与出现集。 零依赖、零 API key、不出图——交付的是提示词，出图在下游。 Use when asked to 场景设定、出场景、环境设定集、场景一致性、scene bibles for AI short drama。 ([zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhang-ai-shortdrama-skills](https://github.com/zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhang-ai-shortdrama-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chuanzhang-changjing) · ★41)
- **chuanzhang-chuangzuo-v1**: v1 版本。中文影视编剧整合技能。用于从零构思概念短片、短片、长片、多集剧集，也用于已有梗概、分场大纲、剧本片段的诊断、量化评分、精准修改、对白打磨、节奏删减和剧本医生。整合中文影视创作流程、视听化写作、分步骤自检机制、经典结构审计、场景价值转变、因果性审计和精准 edit 机制。只输出中文，不使用 Hollywood 格式、docx 输出或双语台词。 ([zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhangAIshijie](https://github.com/zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhangAIshijie/tree/HEAD/skills/chuanzhang-chuangzuo-v1) · ★80)
- **chuanzhang-dagang**: 把一本小说改编成短剧大纲五件套：改编说明、人物表、爽点表、分集梗概、资产清单， 产出 outline.json + Markdown + 单页评审报告（KPI 带、关键决策、爽点时间轴、调度矩阵、场景概览、质量门）。 14 道质量门全部由脚本确定性检查（角色分档上限、主场景上限随集数动态、爽点间隔≤3集、每集钩子悬念必填……），不靠模型自觉； 支持体检模式：贴一份现成大纲进来，只跑质量门给诊断。 零依赖、零 API key，用当前会话额度。 Use when asked to 改编大纲、短剧大纲、拆大纲、小说转短剧、adaptation outline。 ([zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhang-ai-shortdrama-skills](https://github.com/zhangxiansheng-888/chuanzhang-ai-shortdrama-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chuanzhang-dagang) · ★41)

- **chunchi-structure**: 当用户面对一场多强博弈——自己是链条上的一环，强敌正在逐个击破别人，要判断「这一环丢了，下一环是不是轮到自己」、该救该拒、该联该观望时使用。 把《資治通鑑》周紀里四处唇齿叙事（张孟谈说反韩魏、黄歇止秦攻楚、周子谏齐救赵、孔斌警魏大夫）收成一套四步拆法：画「谁是谁的屏障」结构图 → 检索前史同类结构做类比 → 问「敌若胜，下一个是谁」→ 落在救/不救、亲/拒的时点选择。 适用：行业竞争里救伙还是旁观、联盟该不该结、供应链上下游被逐个击破时的自保、地缘与商业博弈推演。 不做：预测胜负吉凶、给历史人物翻案、把史事当寓言朗诵；也没有第二方时可用的单点分析。 不用于：对手连赢之后开始傲慢、你想利用其骄惰设局——那是 jiao-di-cycle（骄敌循环）的题；本卡只管结构传导与时点，不管强弱转化。 English: Use when a user is one link in a chain under a stronger opponent's divide-and-conquer: map who shields whom, find historical analogues, ask who falls next, then decide rescue-or-abandon and ally-or-reject with a deadline. ([yihexiang/rulai-distill](https://github.com/yihexiang/rulai-distill/tree/HEAD/books/tongjian-wei-bundle/skills/chunchi-structure) · ★2)
- **chunfeng**: 东方智慧思维模型引擎（V1.1.0）。融合毛泽东思想方法论（《矛盾论》《实践论》）、查理芒格多元思维模型、马斯克第一性原理，为复杂问题提供深度分析框架。具备自我学习与迭代能力。触发场景：(1) 用户说"用春风分析"、"春风思维"、"用东方智慧分析"，(2) 用户面临复杂决策、矛盾困境、战略规划问题，(3) 用户说"帮我系统思考"、"深度分析这个问题"、"第一性原理分析"，(4) 需要矛盾分析、实践检验、逆向思考、多维视角时，(5) 用户说"多元思维"、"思维模型应用"、"系统方法论"。 ([LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills](https://github.com/LeoYeAI/agent-master-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/chunfeng) · ★2.2k · 2 copies)
